决赛名额和奖项数量何时确定？＋

在线上评测与复核结束后，根据有效参赛规模、成绩分布、现场容量及奖金预算确定，与入围通知一并公布，最迟决赛前7日明确。入围队伍须在名单公布后48小时内由报名登记邮箱确认到场及到场队员，逾期由候补队伍按排名依次递补。

 现场提供电脑或模型账号吗？＋

不提供。请自备电脑、开发和测试设备、软件环境、Agent、模型账号及算力。官方评测设备仅供技术委员会使用，不作为队伍开发设备。

### 具体赛题技术答疑

8 项

商城搜索题（cml-combo-search、cml-spec24）在 x86\_64 模拟器上安装失败，修改 abiFilters 也无效，怎么办？＋

工程依赖的支付 SDK 中有两份原生库只提供 ARM64 版本，修改 `abiFilters` 不能生成 x86\_64 版本，因此原始包无法安装到 x86\_64 模拟器。

 本地调试：针对 cml-combo-search、cml-spec24，可在独立调试副本中隔离这两份原生库，用于搜索功能自测，并标注支付功能不可用。
 正式提交：保留原有支付依赖及相关代码，恢复仅为 x86 调试所做的配置（如 `abiFilters`），不降低 SDK 版本。从基线到提交版本的变更差异中不应包含调试用的隔离改动。
 材料要求：成果包中不提交调试工程及调试安装包，但应保留并提交调试改动差异、构建及自测记录，注明自测设备的架构和限制。
 cml-spec17 需要登录和地址、订单等服务数据，是否需要补齐登录、注册或自行运营后端？＋

本题不要求补齐短信登录、注册或第三方授权。登录及相关服务数据属于场景前置条件，正式考核环境会统一准备。本地自测可以使用独立模拟服务或调试环境。

 云音乐提示“应用不提供在线服务”，README 中的帮助链接无法访问，在线服务怎么配置？＋ [...] #### 如何核验

 线上、线下均须对所有队伍提交计分的每道题完成AI参与证据基础核验，确认原始记录对应本题且体现实际研发参与，不得仅检查文件是否存在。
 对拟入围、递补及有具体疑点的队伍进一步核查完整过程；决赛对全部队伍核查。
 依据原始记录、源码及第三方来源核查章程第五条的执行情况，核验不另设主观加减分。

#### 记录缺失如何处理

 对过程记录缺失须逐题核查：个别记录缺失，但冻结成果包内其他原始证据足以核验本题AI参与及相关来源、修改过程的，按实际测试结果正常计分，不因该缺失直接认定作弊。
 经给予队伍说明机会并核查包内已有证据，仍无法证明某题实际使用AI参与研发的，该题全部F-point均不计通过，仍计入总数，不直接将其他题目或整队成绩归零。
 涉嫌伪造、故意隐瞒或其他违规的，按第11.1条、第11.4条查实处理；不得仅凭缺失推定存在故意行为。
 处理决定须列明涉及题目、事实依据及计分后果，并告知申诉渠道。

#### 截止后可以做什么

 截止后，队伍可应核查要求解释已冻结材料并指出包内已有证据的位置，但不得补交代码、原本漏交的解题材料或以新产生的记录替代原始解题证据；解释须留档，不能作为新增成果参与评分。
 邮件收件、官方故障、计分错误等程序性申诉证据可按第10.3条提交，不属于新增参赛成果。
 评测分与未完成任务如何计算？＋

评测分＝通过的F-point数÷该阶段F-point总数×100。F-point（Feature Checkpoint，可执行功能检查点）指Spec归一化后对应的一个可判定Hypium测试方法。未提交、未完成、构建或安装失败、崩溃、超时、跳

商城、音乐和 WebDAV 题目的外部服务、账号和测试数据如何准备？＋

队伍可使用自建服务和可复现数据开展自测，并可在成果中保留服务源码、启动及初始化说明；但正式评测不依赖队伍自建服务在评测期间持续在线，也不要求队伍提交真实账号、口令、API 密钥等敏感信息。赛事不另行向队伍提供公共测试服务或测试账号；接口路径、参数和响应格式以基线工程既有数据访问代码的约定为准。正式评测使用官方受控的服务、数据或测试适配条件，并通过基线已有的数据访问机制接入：请经由基线已有的网络数据源接口或 API 模块发起请求，保持其方法签名及请求、响应模型兼容；服务地址沿用基线已有的配置及存储方式（如在线服务设置、WebDAV 配置页）。不要将自建服务地址硬编码为数据源，不要另建绕开既有机制的网络通路，也不要依赖新增的构建配置或配置文件切换服务。没有现成配置页面的工程，无需为此新增面向用户的配置页。

队伍仍须完成 Spec 规定的请求调用、返回结果处理和用户可见状态，不能仅显示固定的成功提示。WebDAV 题目中明确写明“向配置的 WebDAV 端点发起请求”的场景，必须产生可观察的实际请求；只改变页面加载状态不满足要求。

 cmu-spec9 的悬浮歌词必须跨应用显示吗？＋

不要求跨应用或系统级全局覆盖显示，不需要为本题申请特殊系统签名或全局悬浮窗权限。在应用前台范围内使用原生子窗口或等效的 ArkUI 窗口完成 Spec 规定的显示、位置调节与恢复、透明度和背景模糊效果即可。正式评测环境无需为本题额外准备权限或签名。

 jm-spec35 需要通过应用市场完成更新吗？＋
 cmu-spec34 中“无缝衔接播放”的含义是什么？＋ [...] 云音乐提示“应用不提供在线服务”，README 中的帮助链接无法访问，在线服务怎么配置？＋

假设服务监听电脑的 `3000` 端口，且模拟器可以通过 `10.0.2.2` 访问电脑，在应用的 API 设置页中填写：

云音乐本地服务配置示例

| 字段 | 填写内容 |
| 主机 | `10.0.2.2` |
| 端口 | `3000` |
| 请求 | HTTP |
| 路径 | 无部署前缀时留空 |
| 用户、密码 | 服务未配置认证时留空 |

如果页面只有一个地址输入框，请填写 ` IP；须确保设备与电脑网络互通，服务监听地址及防火墙允许设备访问。

不同题目的基线对接口的要求可能不同，不能保证任意同名开源 API 服务都兼容。请勿为了让连接状态显示“正常”而跳过服务检查，也不要在提交的源码中写死个人服务器地址。

 cml-spec7 需要完成真实的第三方授权、短信或账号登录吗？＋

不需要为赛题另行申请微信、QQ或支付宝 App ID，不要求完成真实平台授权、服务端授权码交换或取得真实账号的应用登录状态，也不要求准备第三方客户端、测试账号或相关密钥。请按 Spec 完成登录主页上的页面元素、入口及题面明确要求的页面跳转和交互；QQ、支付宝不另行要求完整授权登录。“手机号登录”和“账号密码登录”在本题中要求进入相应页面，不要求真实发送短信或完成真实账号登录。

赛题没有统一指定的《用户协议》《隐私政策》正文或公开链接。队伍可使用与本参赛实现相适的说明文本；页面须按 Spec 展示相应文案和查看入口，正式评测不比对固定正文、运营主体信息或指定 URL。

import { P100 } from "designsystem";

/**
 * @file 网络图片组件（仅设置背景色与圆角）
 * @author Joker.X
 */
@ComponentV2
export struct NetWorkImage {
  /**
   * 图片资源（URL / Resource / PixelMap）
   */
  @Param
  model: string | Resource | PixelMap | null = null;
  /**
   * 图片大小（宽高相等）
   */
  @Param
  sizeValue: Length | undefined = undefined;
  /**
   * 图片缩放模式
   */
  @Param
  imageFit: ImageFit = ImageFit.Cover;
  /**
   * 是否显示背景色
   */
  @Param
  showBackground: boolean = false;
  /**
   * 背景颜色
   */
  @Param
  backgroundColorValue: ResourceColor = $r("app.color.bg_content");
  /**
   * 圆角大小
   */
  @Param
  cornerRadius: Length | undefined = undefined;

  /**
   * 构建网络图片组件
   * @returns {void} 无返回值
   */
  build(): void {
    Stack() {
      if (this.showBackground) {
        Column()
          .width(P100)
          .height(P100)
          .backgroundColor(this.backgroundColorValue)
          .borderRadius(this.cornerRadius ?? 0);
      }

      if (this.model) {
        Image(this.model)
          .objectFit(this.imageFit)
          .borderRadius(this.cornerRadius ?? 0);
      }
    }
    .width(this.sizeValue)
    .height(this.sizeValue)
    .borderRadius(this.cornerRadius ?? 0);
  }
}

# test-harness：通用自测框架

目标：为每道题提供**统一**的设备连接、环境校验、Hypium 封装和测试运行/报告方式；
各题自己的测试点（断言逻辑）单独编写，放在各题工程的 `hypium_tests/` 下，不属于本框架。

本框架不是官方评测器，只用于本地自测，帮助在提交前用同款工具链（Hypium + hdc）验证功能，
降低"本地通过、正式评测翻车"的概率。官方评测环境规格（Pura 90 Pro Max 模拟器、HarmonyOS 6.1.0 / API 23、
1308 × 2880 / 560 dpi）已内置在 `harness/env_check.py` 中。

## 目录结构

```
test-harness/
├── README.md                本文件
├── requirements.txt         框架自身的 Python 依赖（hypium==6.1.0.210）
└── harness/                 通用库，与赛题无关
    ├── __init__.py
    ├── device.py             设备发现、选择、连接（封装 hdc / UiDriver.connect）
    ├── env_check.py          环境校验：对照官方模拟器规格，检查屏幕分辨率/dpi/系统版本
    ├── driver.py             HarnessTestCase：带超时重试的查找/等待/断言辅助方法
    ├── window.py             按窗口名从 hidumper 读取 [x y w h]（悬浮窗等不在 UI 树里的窗口）
    ├── app.py                应用生命周期与导航：restart_app、click_text_path
    ├── runner.py            
...[truncated 1574 chars; full text in native blob c8641f91ecb3ae0d11c72ff9b514bcf1c0d0ca61bda0cce1eb37589b04518743]
```

"""UiDriver 的薄封装基类：带超时重试的查找/等待/断言辅助方法。

各题的测试用例应继承 `HarnessTestCase`，只编写该题 spec 描述的断言逻辑；
设备连接、重试查找、报告格式等通用部分由本框架负责。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time
import unittest

from hypium import BY, UiDriver  # type: ignore[import-not-found]

from . import device as device_mod

DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 15
DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.5


class HarnessTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    """所有题目的 Hypium 测试类的通用基类。

    子类可直接使用 `self.driver`（已连接的 UiDriver）、
    `self.require_text()`、`self.require_component()`、`self.find_or_none()`。
    设备序列号通过类属性 `device_sn` 传入（由 runner 负责设置），
    若单独运行某个测试文件，也可以设置环境变量 HDC_DEVICE 或在命令行传 --device。
    """

    device_sn: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
        sn = cls.device_sn or device_mod.select_device(None)
        cls.driver = UiDriver.connect(device_sn=sn)
        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(5)

    def find_or_none(self, by, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
        """按 `by` 条件轮询查找组件，超时返回 None（不触发失败）。"""
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            component = self.driver.find_component(by)
            if component is not None:
                return component
            self.driver.wait(DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL)
        return None

    def require_component(self, by, message: str, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
        """按 `by` 条件轮询查找组件，超时则用 `message` 调用 self.fail()。"""
        component 
...[truncated 6248 chars; full text in native blob 801f44dd69cb11939f08372503295f9f0708919cf8e881adf5d63a9b60c35a5b]
```

> reasoning: I'm planning out the component files needed for the goods feature — a comment item component for reviews with nickname/rating/thumbnails, a comment card wrapping the list, and a detail title component.
> 
> I also need a detail image component and a fullscreen comment image viewer using Swiper with an indicator and close button. For the GoodsDetailPage, I'm thinking through how to integrate a comment card ListItem and detail images into the main List — and realizing that using ForEach for potentially 100 content images would build all nodes upfront rather than lazily, which could strain memory and network, so I may need LazyForEach with a proper IDataSource instead.
> 
> I'm weighing whether LazyForEach works cleanly with ComponentV2, or whether Repeat with virtualScroll (the newer API 12+ approach) is more appropriate for lazy loading inside a List, though I'm unsure about its compatibility when mixed with other ListItem children. Ultimately I need to make sure the test framework can still locate elements even if off-screen nodes aren't fully laid out, which should be fine since tests scroll into view anyway.
> 
> I'm also thinking about what an official test would likely do: scroll to find review sections, verify nickname/rating/content, locate interactive elements like "+1", scroll to product details, check images, click thumbnails, swipe through them, and verify scroll position afterward. For performance with many images, LazyForEach could work as a mixed child within List alongside other ListItems, rebuilding the data source once the underlying data becomes available.
> 
> But since LazyForEach needs to be a direct child of List for laziness to apply, I'm reconsidering whether the original implementation just used ForEach instead, since List with ForEach creates all children upfront regardless. I'm also weighing whether ArkUI's Image component defers decoding until layout/paint even if download starts earlier at creation time.
> 
> For robustness I'm leaning toward LazyForEach despite the extra complexity of implementing an IDataSource class, especially since the page's data can change on retry—I'd need to rebuild the datasource reactively, perhaps using a Monitor, or consider Repeat with virtual scrolling as an alternative.
> 
> Checking the docs on mixing Repeat's virtualScroll mode with other list children, though, it seems mixing ListItem, ForEach, and LazyForEach within the same scroll container isn't recommended, and neither is using multiple Repeat components together—so I need to figure out whether LazyForEach mixed with plain ListItem is actually safe here.
> 
> I'm considering putting detail images under a ListItemGroup with a header for the "商品详情" title, followed by LazyForEach children, though a simpler approach of a plain title ListItem followed by LazyForEach items might work just as well. Since 100 is likely just a cap and not the typical dataset size, ForEach might actually be simpler and sufficient here.

import { NetworkResponse } from "model";
import { RequestHelper } from "result";
import { BaseViewModel } from "./BaseViewModel";
import { BaseNetWorkUiState } from "../state/BaseNetWorkUiState";

/**
 * @file 网络请求 ViewModel 基类
 * @description 提供通用的网络请求 ViewModel 基类，包含 UI 状态管理和请求执行逻辑。
 * @param T 网络请求数据项类型
 * @author Joker.X
 */
@ObservedV2
export abstract class BaseNetWorkViewModel<T> extends BaseViewModel {
  /**
   * 通用网络请求 UI 状态
   */
  @Trace
  uiState: BaseNetWorkUiState = BaseNetWorkUiState.LOADING;
  /**
   * 请求成功后的数据
   */
  @Trace
  data: T | null = null;
  /**
   * 控制请求失败时是否显示 Toast 提示
   */
  protected showErrorToast: boolean = false;

  /**
   * 子类必须实现此方法，返回网络请求 Promise
   * @returns {Promise<NetworkResponse<T>>} 网络请求 Promise
   */
  protected abstract requestRepository(): Promise<NetworkResponse<T>>;

  /**
   * 页面出现时的生命周期回调
   * @returns {void} 无返回值
   */
  aboutToAppear(): void {
    this.executeRequest();
  }

  /**
   * 发起请求
   * @returns {void} 无返回值
   */
  executeRequest(): void {
    RequestHelper.repository<T>(this.requestRepository())
      .toast(this.showErrorToast)
      .start(() => this.onRequestStart())
      .execute()
      .then((data: T) => this.onRequestSuccess(data))
      .catch(() => this.onRequestError());
  }

  /**
   * 请求开始前回调
   * @returns {void} 无返回值
   */
  protected onRequestStart(): void {
    this.setLoadingState();
  }

  /**
   * 请求成功回调
   * @param {T} data - 成功数据
   * @return

 "content": "import { P100 } from \"designsystem\";\n\n/**\n * 无法得知图片尺寸时的占位宽高比\n */\nconst DEFAULT_ASPECT_RATIO: number = 1;\n\n/**\n * @file 商品详情图片组件\n * @author Joker.X\n */\n@ComponentV2\nexport struct GoodsDetailImage {\n  /**\n   * 图片地址\n   */\n  @Param\n  url: string = \"\";\n  /**\n   * 图片序号（用于生成稳定 id）\n   */\n  @Param\n  index: number = 0;\n  /**\n   * 图片加载完成后的真实宽高比\n   */\n  @Local\n  private loadedRatio: number = 0;\n\n  /**\n   * 构建商品详情图片\n   * @returns {void} 无返回值\n   */\n  build(): void {\n    Stack() {\n      Column()\n        .width(P100)\n        .height(P100)\n        .backgroundColor($r(\"app.color.bg_content\"));\n\n      Image(this.url)\n        .id(`goods_detail_image_${this.index}`)\n        .width(P100)\n        .height(P100)\n        .objectFit(ImageFit.Auto)\n        .onComplete((event?: ImageCompleteEvent): void => {\n          if (event && event.width > 0 && event.height > 0) {\n            this.loadedRatio = event.width / event.height;\n          }\n        });\n    }\n    .width(P100)\n    .aspectRatio(this.getAspectRatio());\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * 获取占位宽高比：优先使用加载结果，其次使用地址中的 w/h 参数\n   * @returns {number} 宽高比\n   */\n  private getAspectRatio(): number {\n    if (this.loadedRatio > 0) {\n      return this.loadedRatio;\n    }\n    const width: number = this.getUrlParam(\"w\");\n    const height: number = this.getUrlParam(\"h\");\n    return width > 0 && height > 0 ? width / height : DEFAULT_ASPECT_RATIO;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * 读取地址中的数字参数\n   * @param {string} name - 参数名\n   * @returns {number} 参数值，不存在时为 0\n   */\n  private getUrlParam(name: string): \n...[truncated 1129 chars; full text in native blob 7eabe2d103c3368fe0c3d35635dfe465661c2ee07fd4419490236731a84eb925]",

> reasoning: I'm adding a lazy-loading image data source for the goods detail page, with a monitor watching the view model's data property to refresh the image source whenever the content pics change.
> 
> I'm considering the rendering order between when the data arrives and when the LazyForEach rebuilds -- either sequence should work since setData triggers a reload regardless of timing. I'll keep the title ListItem wrapped in a conditional for non-empty pics, while always rendering the LazyForEach so it just shows nothing when data is empty.
> 
> Each list item wraps a GoodsDetailImage component keyed by URL and index, so reloads diff correctly even when all keys change. For the image viewer, I'm attaching bindContentCover to the List using the visibility flag from the view model, with an alpha transition and a close handler on disappear, following the standard CustomBuilder pattern from the docs where the builder call itself is passed as the second argument.
> 
> Since there's no onWillDismiss handler, a back press should naturally dismiss the cover and trigger closeImageViewer through onDisappear — I should double check that the content cover intercepts the back event before the NavDestination does. Padding at the bottom of the list and safe-area handling for the bottom bar aren't my concern here. For comments, I want to guard against `comment` not being an array before using it, since the current fallback only handles null/undefined.

{
 "command": "mkdir -p /tmp/cm26 && cat > /tmp/cm26/dump.py <<'EOF'\nimport json, subprocess, sys\nHDC = \"/Applications/DevEco-Studio.app/Contents/sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains/hdc\"\ndef sh(c): return subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", \"127.0.0.1:5555\", \"shell\", c], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout\ndef dump():\n    sh(\"rm -f /data/local/tmp/l.json; uitest dumpLayout -p /data/local/tmp/l.json\")\n    subprocess.run([HDC, \"-t\", \"127.0.0.1:5555\", \"file\", \"recv\", \"/data/local/tmp/l.json\", \"/tmp/cm26/l.json\"], capture_output=True)\n    return json.load(open(\"/tmp/cm26/l.json\"))\ndef walk(n, out, depth=0):\n    a = n.get(\"attributes\", {})\n    out.append((depth, a))\n    for c in n.get(\"children\", []): walk(c, out, depth+1)\n    return out\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    flt = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else None\n    for d, a in walk(dump(), []):\n        t, k, i = a.get(\"type\"), a.get(\"text\", \"\"), a.get(\"id\", \"\")\n        if flt and flt not in (t or \"\") + k + i + a.get(\"key\", \"\"): continue\n        if not flt and not (k or i): continue\n        print(f\"{'  '*min(d,30)}{t} text={k!r} id={i!r} key={a.get('key','')!r} b={a.get('bounds')} vis={a.get('visible')} clk={a.get('clickable')} bg={a.get('backgroundColor','')}\")\nEOF\ncd /tmp/cm26 && python3 dump.py | head -60",

export const WINDOW_SAFE_AREA_STATE_KEY: string = "window_safe_area_state";

@ObservedV2

  @Trace
  topInset: number = 0;
  @Trace
  leftInset: number = 0;
  @Trace
  bottomInset: number = 0;
  @Trace
  rightInset: number = 0;

    this.leftInset = leftInset;
    this.bottomInset = bottomInset;
    this.rightInset = rightInset;
  }

  updateSafeAreaByInsets(insets: SafeAreaInsets): void {
    this.updateSafeArea(insets.top, insets.left, insets.bottom, insets.right);
  }
}

export function getWindowSafeAreaState(): WindowSafeAreaState {

    WINDOW_SAFE_AREA_STATE_KEY,
    () => new WindowSafeAreaState()
  )!;

I notice the builder log for "render indicator 0" fires twice, suggesting the builder is being invoked more than once, possibly creating a duplicate CommentImageViewer instance that isn't the one actually displayed.

> I'm also wondering if the NavDestination becomes inactive while covered by a full-screen modal, and whether V2 components default to not freezing when inactive -- that could explain the setter firing without triggering a rerender.
> 
> Stepping back, I'm reconsidering ObserveV2's mechanics: setting a @Local property should call fireChange and mark bound elmtIds dirty, since the Monitor registers at construction and old/new values clearly differ (0 to 1). That should work normally, so maybe the issue is that the dirty flush depends on the active UI instance context, which differs when inside the modal's bindContentCover versus the page itself -- even though closing the modal via the viewmodel's @Trace change works fine through the same mechanism, so there's some inconsistency specific to this component's monitor/dirty handling.
> 
> I'm wondering if the component is being treated as inactive -- isViewActive() returning false -- since V2 skips rerendering for inactive components, and NavDestination propagates hidden state when the page is covered, even without freezeWhenInactive explicitly set. That would explain why both the rerender and the Monitor callback fail to fire, since freeze mode delays Monitor execution too, and I don't see any freezeWhenInactive decorator or NavDestination mode settings that would explain this behavior. I also want to check whether my hilog reading missed filtered-out monitor logs, since the animation and change logs did appear but monitor logs under the same tag might have been dropped by flow control.
> 
> But testing the freezing hypothesis: if I move currentIndex into the view model as a @Trace property passed via @Param, and the close action already works by updating a bindContentCover attribute on the page's List -- which proves the page itself isn't frozen -- then the viewer component failing to rerender can't simply be explained by the whole page being frozen, since the page clearly updates fine.
> 
> I'm wondering if the modal's builder content creates a detached component whose elmtId tracking isn't properly linked to the parent, or if @Monitor callbacks scheduled via a microtask aren't firing for some reason tied to how ObserveV2 schedules dirty updates.
> 
> Now I want to verify whether the setter for currentIndex is actually being invoked — logging the value right after assignment and checking pics.length would clarify if the getter is returning stale data. I'm also double-checking the constructor and resetStateVarsOnReuse initialization of currentIndex to rule out a reset issue, and want to look closer at the generated code around where these assignments happen.

> reasoning: I'm puzzling over why aboutToDisappear fires right after rendering while the frame nodes stay visible — it seems the viewer instance got detached from the V2 tree, disabling rerender. My guess is bindContentCover rebuilds the builder content when the List's attributes change due to vm state, causing the instance to disappear, but I don't see a matching appear log to confirm that.
> 
> Since the viewer is created within the List's update context when `imageViewerVisible` toggles, the List's own re-execution cycle might be treating that child as stale and deleting it — this feels like a known V2 quirk where bindSheet/bindContentCover builders with custom components inside a V2 parent trigger immediate aboutToDisappear. A likely fix is avoiding a custom component inside the builder altogether and instead building the viewer UI directly with native nodes in the page's own @Builder, driven by @Trace state.
> 
> Alternatively, I could try ComponentContent with openBindSheet/openCustomDialog, though that's more complex, or abandon bindContentCover entirely and instead overlay the viewer as a Stack directly in the page when visible, handling back-press through the NavDestination — though that changes the original design decision.
> 
> The simpler path is keeping the viewer as a @Builder with state in the VM alongside bindContentCover, since builder-created components get their update functions registered on the page itself, and the page isn't deleted, so updates should still propagate. I suspect the real issue is that when a custom component gets created inside the List's observeComponentCreation2 closure, it's attributed to the List's elmtId rather than the page, so it gets purged when the List's render cycle completes.
> 
> I could still need a reactive indicator even with this approach, since the builder content binds to vm.imageViewerIndex and the page's observe scope should rerender that element—unless those elmtIds also get purged like before. Worth testing quickly: I'd move the viewer UI into a page-level builder function exported from CommentImageViewer.ets, matching the spec's requirement for a fullscreen viewer triggered on comment image tap.
> 
> The catch is that global @Builder functions pass params by value in V2 (not reactive) unless using a reference param or the newer makeBinding API. The real mystery is why the instance disappears without a corresponding re-appear—if the bindContentCover rebuilt the content, I'd expect a new instance log, but there isn't one, meaning the native nodes stay rendered while the component instance itself gets deleted underneath them.

> reasoning: I'm considering whether the official test uses scrollSearch targeting a List component to find "商品评价", but wondering if there could be ambiguity with multiple List nodes since the home page's list might still linger in the hidden node tree after navigating to the detail page.
> 
> For scrollSearch, I should verify it also works for locating "商品详情" within the LazyForEach-rendered list.
> 
> Now I'm planning out the test file structure: a `test_goods_detail_comments.py` with a helper class, where each test restarts the app, waits for network, and navigates to the Xiaomi 17T Pro detail page via a home screen click. I'm sketching test cases that scroll to find "商品评价", verify it's positioned after the service section, and check that the first review item has a non-empty nickname, a rating value, and review content — possibly cross-checking this against the actual API response fetched from the host to validate correctness.
> 
> I'm also outlining additional test cases: verifying the "+1" badge sits within the thumbnail bounds and to the right of the comment content, handling the case where an item has a single image thumbnail without a count badge, and then scrolling further to confirm the detail title appears after the comment section, followed by checking that detail images render with correct width, positive height, and increasing order as more are scrolled into view. I'm noting that checking placeholder background colors might not be accessible through the standard UI component API, so I may need to skip that verification.
> 
> For the viewer tests, I'm planning scenarios for opening the image viewer, swiping between images and confirming the "x/y" indicator updates correctly at boundaries, closing via the close button and verifying the underlying comment section bounds stay unchanged, closing via back button (first closing only the viewer, then the whole page), reopening to confirm the index resets to 1, switching between comments with different image counts, and testing rapid double-clicks on a thumbnail to ensure only a single viewer opens.
> 
> I also want a test confirming double-clicking close doesn't pass through to the underlying page, and a network test that clears hilog, performs viewer interactions, and checks no goodsDetail NetLog requests fire while the viewer is open — with a positive control verifying exactly one such request fires when the detail page itself loads.
> 
> For remaining tests: verify re-entering the detail page after backing out or restarting shows no stray "1/2" state, scroll through all comment images via fling gestures to confirm the last index matches the API's contentPics count without crashing, and check a product with no comment images (830016) handles gracefully.
> 
> I need to figure out if 830016 is reachable from the home screen, maybe buried in the "全部商品" grid, so I'll try scrollSearch for "REDMI Note 17 Pro" or just pull the API data directly for both 830001 and 830016 to see what's on home. The retry path is tough to test offline so I'll skip it and note that in the report.

> reasoning: I'll count request lines matching the goods detail pattern while excluding response entries, and plan to verify this against actual hilog output after opening a detail page. For the test file, I'm structuring it around the harness's test case class with app restart support, building reusable helper functions at the module level, and designing a base class to keep the network flows stable against flakiness by waiting for DNS resolution before restarting.
> 
> The base class will include network readiness checks, a home-launch routine with retry logic for network error states, a detail-opening helper that searches and scrolls for the target product, plus utilities for scrolling to specific elements, grabbing bounds, and opening the image viewer. For the check that the review section appears after the service section, I'm working out the vertical positions of both sections from the UI dump so I can scroll to a point where both are visible for comparison.
> 
> For verifying ratings, the plan is to grab the displayed rating text and compare it numerically against the star count from the API—fetching the same public backend the app itself uses for goods detail. For checking review ordering, I'll compare the nickname and content of the first several items against the API's comment list, scrolling as needed to locate each by its content key. For the detail images test, I'm checking that each image's position comes after the title, its width matches the list width, and comparing placeholder heights before and after images load.
> 
> Since image height ratios are hard to validate reliably when images get clipped by the list's visible bounds, I'll loosen that check to just confirm width and positive height rather than exact pixel ratios. Scrolling through all 44 detail images one by one would take too many swipes, so I'm considering using a targeted scroll search to jump directly toward the last image instead.
> 
> I should also verify that after scrolling to bottom, the last image's bottom edge stays within the list bounds, not hidden behind the bottom bar. For network logging, I'm considering toggling hilog's flow control off before the test and restoring it in tearDown, since that affected log visibility in earlier debugging. I'll move forward with writing the actual test file now.

The work in [`1-do.md`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cm-spec26/spec/1-do.md) is finished. All 17 Hypium self-tests passed on the emulator, and a clean build gives exactly the same ArkTS warnings as the baseline (234), so nothing new was introduced.

**What I implemented** (all in `feature/goods`):
- **Five new components:**
- `GoodsCommentItem`: nickname, a read-only star rating, the review text, and a thumbnail on the right with a "+N" badge.
- `GoodsCommentCard`: the "商品评价" card.
- `GoodsDetailTitle`: the "商品详情" divider title.
- `GoodsDetailImage`: full-width detail images using `ImageFit.Auto`. A background placeholder is shown, sized from the w/h values in the image URL.
- `CommentImageViewer`: full-screen swipeable viewer with a "1/2"-style page number and a "关闭" button.
- **Detail images** load lazily via `LazyForEach`, using a data source in `model/GoodsDetailImageDataSource.ets`.
- **Wiring:** `GoodsDetailPage` adds these to the existing detail list after the service card. The viewer opens with `bindContentCover`. `GoodsDetailViewModel` holds the viewer state, blocks a second open on repeated taps, and handles empty data.
- **No new requests.** Reviews and images both come from the existing `page/goodsDetail` response, and I didn't touch models, APIs, routes or shared base components. All UI text reuses existing string resources. Every stable id listed in section 7 of [`acceptance.md`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cm-spec26/spec/acceptance.md) is in place.

**Bug found on the device:** when a custom component is the root of the `bindContentCover` content, it gets destroyed right after it's created. The page number then stayed at "1/2" after swiping. Wrapping it in a `Column` fixed it, and there's a code comment explaining why.

**What the tests cover:**
- The review section sits after the service info.
- Nickname, rating and text match the server data in order.
- "+1" and "+2" badges appear where expected.
- Reviews without images show no thumbnail.
- The detail title sits after the reviews, and images keep server order and full width, scrolling all the way to the last one.
- The viewer opens, swipes, stops at the last image, resets when reopened, and shows "1/1" for a single image.
- After closing, the scroll position is unchanged.
- Back closes only the viewer.
- Double-tapping a thumbnail or the close button causes no problems.
- Entering the page sends exactly one request, and the viewer sends none.
- Going back and re-entering, restarting the app, and fast scrolling all work.

`logs/reports/` also keeps two earlier failed runs. Both failures were bugs in my test script, not the app:
- The shared harness's `restart_app` couldn't find the app's main ability, so the tests restart the app themselves.
- My script's count of network requests from the log was parsed wrongly.

**Not verified on the device:**
- **Empty-data cases:** blank nickname showing "匿名用户", no reviews, and no detail images. The public server has no such products, so these were only checked by reading the code.
- **Request failure and retry:** I didn't simulate a network outage. The baseline's "重试" button may not respond to taps, which was already accepted as a risk.
- **Products with 100 detail images** were never scrolled to the end. The 44-image product works fine.
- **Emulator DNS fails intermittently.** The tests wait for the network first and record a network failure as skipped, not passed.

All of this is in [`spec/implementation-notes.md`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cm-spec26/spec/implementation-notes.md) for [`2-submit.md`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cm-spec26/spec/2-submit.md). `git status` shows only real changes, with no build noise.

## Skill: comate-session-ids

**Base directory**: /Users/bress/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids


# 查询当前会话 ID

## 为什么要分两步

引擎 sessionUuid 只能从 hook 日志里拿：每次工具调用**结束后**，日志会写一条带 `conversationId` 和会话 `cwd` 的 PostToolUse 记录。一条命令执行时看不到它自己的记录，所以：

1. 第一步跑一个打时间戳的命令，它结束后日志里就有一条"本会话"的记录；
2. 第二步找"时间戳之后、本工作区的第一条记录"，那就是第一步的记录，ID 一定属于当前会话。

不要改成"取该工作区最近一条记录"：同一工作区开着别的会话时，最近一条往往是别人的，这正是旧版的 bug。用斜杠命令（`/comate-session-ids`）加载本 skill 时不产生工具调用，同样不能依赖"调用 skill 的记录"。

## 步骤

两步必须是**两次独立的工具调用**，不要用 `&&`、`;` 合成一条，也不要并行。

1. 打时间戳：

   ```bash
   python3 ~/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids/scripts/session_ids.py --stamp
   ```

   输出一个整数，比如 `1792229799425`。

2. 紧接着查询（工作区用绝对路径；Agent 的 cwd 可能是 `/`，不要依赖它）：

   ```bash
   python3 ~/.comate/skills/comate-session-ids/scripts/session_ids.py "<工作区绝对路径>" --after <上一步的整数>
   ```

   需要结构化结果时加 `--json`。脚本只读：读 hook 日志，用 `mode=ro` 打开 IDE 状态库，不写任何文件。

3. 按输出回答用户：
   - `engine sessionUuid`：正常情况下 `hook entry` 在时间戳后几秒内，可直接采信。若出现 `WARNING other sessions logged tool calls in the same window`，说明同一时刻别的会话也在调用工具，把候选列给用户；也可以重做一遍两步，通常就不再冲突。
   - `sidebar session ID`：取状态为 `generating`（正在运行）且唯一的那条，附带标题方便用户核对。
   - 一句话说明依据来源（见下文"数据来源"）。

## 结果不完整时

- `engine ... NOT FOUND (no PostToolUse entry ... after the stamp)`：hook 可能写得慢，再执行一次第 2 步（不用重新打戳）。还是没有就如实告诉用户，不要改用其他办法猜。
- `engine ... NOT FOUND (hook log not found)`：本机没有启用这个 hook，告知用户即可。
- `sidebar session ID: UNDETERMINED`：同时有多个或没有 `generating` 会话。把列出的候选（ID、状态、更新时间、标题）给用户，按标题让用户确认，不要自己挑。
- `sidebar ... NOT FOUND`，或候选里没有当前会话：这张表只记录
...[truncated 849 chars; full text in native JSON]

当前会话的引擎 sessionUuid 查到了，侧边栏会话 ID 没找到。

- **引擎 sessionUuid**：`d24c0c2a-83d8-4b07-a3c0-854df3e0361a`。来自 hook 日志 `~/.comate/builtin-hook-state/notify-dispatcher.log`：先打一个时间戳，取它之后本工作区的第一条 PostToolUse 记录，这条记录在时间戳后 0.2 秒写入，没有其他会话的记录与它冲突，可以直接用。
- **IDE 侧边栏会话 ID**：本工作区 IDE 状态库里没有“正在运行”的 Spec 会话记录，也没有候选。这张表只记录 Spec 模式的会话，普通 Agent 会话一般没有。普通会话的侧边栏 ID 是否就等于引擎 sessionUuid，没有验证过，不能当成同一个值。

填 [`2-submit.md`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cm-spec26/spec/2-submit.md) 第二节的会话表时，sessionUuid 一栏可以填上面的值。“IDE 侧边栏会话 ID（可选）”一栏，请你在侧边栏核对后自己填，否则留空。
